# Bök Thesis Terms Matched Against Website Corpus

This report cross-references terms mined from Christian Bök’s 1997 dissertation and the site’s Pataphysics vocabulary against the local Elden Glass website corpus (`content`, `data`, `app`, `components`, `lib`).

## Summary

- Candidate thesis/vocabulary terms searched: 636
- Terms with at least one local website match: 591
- Terms without local website matches: 45
